### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the displacement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> and velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>)</mo></math> of a particle executing SHM are related through the expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>. If the time period of the particle is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt></math>, then what is the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math>?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **12.00**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given equation,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
Differentiating both sides, we get
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mfrac><mi>dv</mi><mi>dx</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mfrac><mi>dv</mi><mi>dx</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>v</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><msqrt><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mfrac><mtext>&#160;acceleration&#160;</mtext><mtext>&#160;displacement&#160;</mtext></mfrac></mfenced></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><msqrt><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><msqrt><mn>12</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"/></p>

</details>

---

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An ideal gas is enclosed in a vertical cylindrical container and supports a freely moving piston of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math>. The piston and the cylinder have an equal cross-sectional area <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>. Atmospheric pressure is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>p</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> and when the piston is in equilibrium, the volume of the gas is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>. The piston is now displaced slightly from its equilibrium position. Assuming that the system, is completely isolated from, its surroundings,  what is the frequency of oscillation. An ideal gas is enclosed in a vertical cylindrical container and supports a freely moving piston of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math>. The piston and the cylinder have an equal cross-sectional area <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>. Atmospheric pressure is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>p</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> and when the piston is in equilibrium, the volume of the gas is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>. The piston is now displaced slightly from its equilibrium position. Assuming that the system, is completely isolated from, its surroundings,  what is the frequency of oscillation.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;&#160;</mi><mfenced><mrow><msub><mtext>p</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mtext>A</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>MgA</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>M</mtext></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>&gamma;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>Mg</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>Mg</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>A</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>Mg</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;&#160;</mi><mfenced><mrow><msub><mtext>p</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mtext>A</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>MgA</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msub><mtext>V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>M</mtext></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In equilibrium, pressure inside the cylinder = pressure just outside it

or   <math> <mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>Mg</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

When piston is displaced slightly by an amount x, change in volume,

  <math> <mrow> <mtext>dV</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>Ax</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Since, the cylinder is isolated from the surroundings, nature of the process is adiabatic. In adiabatic process,

  <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>dp</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>dV</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mi>γ</mi> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>V</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>


<span class="Bodytext2BookAntiqua"><span style="font-size: 8.5pt; color: black;"><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0007517_AnsExp1_1.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 187px; height: 241px;" /></span></span>

or increase in pressure inside the cylinder,
 
<math> <mrow> <mtext>dp</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>γ</mi> <mtext>p</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>V</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>dV</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mi>γ</mi> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>Mg</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>Ax</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math> ​

This increase in pressure when multiplied with area of cross-section A will give a net upward force (or the restoring force). Hence,

<math> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>dp</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>A</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mi>γ</mi> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>MgA</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Or <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mi>γ</mi> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>MgA</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Since, <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>∝</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </math>, motion of the piston is simple harmonic in nature. Frequency of this oscillation is given by

<math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </msqrt> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>γ </mi> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msup> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>MgA</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi></math> tube of the uniform bore of cross-sectional area <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> is set up vertically with open ends. A liquid of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math> and density <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi></math> is poured into it. The liquid column will oscillate with a period

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow>  <mn>2</mn>  <mi>&pi;</mi>  <msqrt>    <mfrac>      <mrow>        <mtext>M</mtext>      </mrow>      <mrow>        <mtext>g</mtext>      </mrow>    </mfrac>  </msqrt></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow>  <mn>2</mn>  <mi>&pi;</mi>  <msqrt>    <mfrac>      <mrow>        <mtext>M</mtext>        <mtext>A</mtext>      </mrow>      <mrow>        <mtext>d</mtext>        <mtext>g</mtext>      </mrow>    </mfrac>  </msqrt></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow>  <mn>2</mn>  <mi>&pi;</mi>  <msqrt>    <mfrac>      <mrow>        <mtext>M</mtext>      </mrow>      <mrow>        <mtext>A</mtext>        <mtext>d</mtext>        <mtext>g</mtext>      </mrow>    </mfrac>  </msqrt></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>A</mtext> <mtext>d</mtext> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>A</mtext> <mtext>d</mtext> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

Where L is the length of the liquid is one of the limbs. However, if L is taken to be the length of the liquid column, then the length of liquid in each limb is <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></math>.

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒   </mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒   </mo> <mtext>M</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>AL</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>d</mtext> <mo>  ⇒  </mo> <mtext>L</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>Ad</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒   </mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>Adg</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A horizontal rod of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> and length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> is pivoted at one end. The rod at the other end is supported by a spring of force constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></math>. The rod is displaced by a small angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> from its horizontal equilibrium position and released. The time period (in second) of the subsequent simple harmonic motion is


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image9-1065fb07-0424-4775-87ad-f0abcf09effa.jpg" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">E=</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

At equilibrium,

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>2 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle executes simple harmonic motion and it is located at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>b</mi></math> and <math><mi>c</mi></math> at time <math><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi> </mi><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> respectively. The frequency of the oscillation is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ω</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>ω</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>ω</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ω</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>ω</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>ω</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ω</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></math>

<math> <mo>∵</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>sin</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>D</mi> </math>

<math> <mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mfrac> <mrow> <mi>C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>D</mi> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mi>cos</mi><mfrac> <mrow> <mi>C</mi><mo>−</mo><mi>D</mi> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </math>

<math><mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle performs SHM with an amplitude of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. If at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>, the magnitude of velocity and acceleration are equal, then the time period of SHM (in seconds) is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><msqrt><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#969;</mi></math>

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#969;</mi></math>

<math><mi>&#969;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A uniform rod of mass m and length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> is hinged at one of its end with the ceiling and another end of the rod is attached with a thread which is attached with the horizontal ceiling at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>. If one end of the rod is slightly displaced horizontally and perpendicular to the rod and released. If the time period of small oscillation is <math><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>. Find <math><mi>x</mi><mo>.</mo></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/642358_ques.png" style="width: 200px; height: 104px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

Consider a small angular displacement <math><mi>β</mi></math>

Torque about the rotational axis

<math><mi>τ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>τ</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>I</mi><mi>α</mi></math>

<math><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mi>α</mi></math>

<math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>β</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A body executes simple harmonic motion under the action of a force, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> with a time period <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>. If the force is changed to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>, it executes SHM with time period <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>. If both the forces <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> act simultaneously in the same direction on the body, its time period (in seconds) is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>12</mn><mn>25</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>24</mn><mn>25</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>35</mn><mn>24</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>25</mn><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>12</mn><mn>25</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mfrac><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>m</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mfrac><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>m</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi>x</mi></math></p><p>Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mi>x</mi></math></p><p>Now, resultant force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>T</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msubsup><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msubsup><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><msqrt><msubsup><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></msqrt></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mrow><msqrt><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>12</mn><mn>25</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block of mass <em><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math></em> is attached to a cart of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> through spring of spring constant <em><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></math></em> as shown in the figure. Friction is absent everywhere. The time period of oscillations of the system, when spring is compressed and then released, is

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/caa732af-6003-4417-a055-3f7cce6c4811-image2.jpeg" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

It is equivalent to a system as shown below,

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3e2087a7-d7ed-4127-80df-7e09dd5be30e-image3.jpeg" />


<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi>reduced</mi></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

</details>
